( _newline_   cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_   playlist: RundownPlaylist, _newline_   horizontalDelta: number, _newline_   verticalDelta: number, _newline_   setManually: boolean, _newline_   nextPartId0?: PartId _newline_  ): PartId | null { _newline_  _newline_   if (!playlist.active) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" is not active!`) _newline_  _newline_   if (playlist.holdState && playlist.holdState !== RundownHoldState.COMPLETE) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" cannot change next during hold!`) _newline_  _newline_ _newline_    previousPartInstance _newline_   } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_  _newline_   let currentNextPart: DBPart _newline_   if (nextPartId0) { _newline_ Segment _newline_   if (!currentNextSegment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${currentNextPart.segmentId}" not found!`) _newline_  _newline_   const partsInSegments: {[segmentId: string]: Part[]} = {} _newline_   _.each(segments, segment => { _newline_    let partsInSegment = _.filter(parts, p => p.segmentId === segment._id) _newline_    if (partsInSegment.length) { _newline_     partsInSegments[unprotectString(segment._id)] = partsInSegment _newline_     parts.push(...partsInSegment) _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  _newline_   let partIndex: number = -1 _newline_   _.find(parts, (part, i) => { _newline_    if (part._id === currentNextPart._id) { _newline_     partIndex = i _newline_     return true _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_   let segmentIndex: number = -1 _newline_   _.find(segments, (s, i) => { _newline_    if (s._id === currentNextSegment._id) { _newline_     segmentIndex = i _newline_     return true _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_   if (partIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part not found in list of parts!`) _newline_   if (segmentIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${currentNextSegment._id}" not found in segmentsWithParts!`) _newline_   if (verticalDelta !== 0) { _newline_    segmentIndex += verticalDelta _newline_  _newline_    const segment = segments[segmentIndex] _newline_    if (!segment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `No Segment found!`) _newline_  _newline_    const part = _.first(partsInSegments[unprotectString(segment._id)]) _newline_    if (!part) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `No Parts in segment "${segment._id}"!`) _newline_  _newline_    partIndex = -1 _newline_    _.find(parts, (p, i) => { _newline_     if (p._id === part._id) { _newline_      partIndex = i _newline_      return true _newline_     } _newline_    }) _newline_    if (partIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part (from segment) not found in list of parts!`) _newline_   } _newline_   partIndex += horizontalDelta _newline_  _newline_   partIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(parts.length - 1, partIndex)) _newline_  _newline_   let part = parts[partIndex] _newline_   if (!part) !part.isPlayable()) { _newline_    // Whoops, we're not allowed to next to that. _newline_    // Skip it, then (ie run the whole thing again) _newline_    if (part._id !== nextPartId0) { _newline_ _newline_   } else { _newline_    setNextPartInner(cache, playlist, part, setManually) _newline_    return part._id _newline_   } _newline_  } _newline_  export function setNextSegment ( _newline_ _newline_  ) { _newline_   if (!playlist.active) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `Rundown Playlist "${playlist._id}" is not active!`) _newline_   if (playlist.holdState && playlist.holdState !== rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(rundownPlaylistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_    const dbPlaylist = checkAccessAndGetPlaylist(context, rundownPlaylistId) _newline_    if (!horizontalDelta && !verticalDelta) throw new Meteor.Error(402, `rundownMoveNext: invalid delta: (${horizontalDelta}, ${verticalDelta})`) _newline_  _newline_    const cache = Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_    const res = moveNextPartInner( _newline_     cache, _newline_     playlist, _newline_     horizontalDelta, _newline_     verticalDelta, _newline_     setManually _newline_    ) _newline_    waitForPromise(cache.saveAllToDatabase()) _newline_    return res _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_  function moveNextPartInner ( _newline_   cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_   playlist: RundownPlaylist, _newline_   horizontalDelta: number, _newline_   verticalDelta: number, _newline_   setManually: boolean, _newline_   nextPartId0?: PartId _newline_  ): PartId | null { _newline_  _newline_   if (!playlist.active) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" is not active!`) _newline_  _newline_   if (playlist.holdState && playlist.holdState !== RundownHoldState.COMPLETE) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" cannot change next during hold!`) _newline_  _newline_   const { segments, parts } = getSegmentsAndPartsFromCache(cache, playlist) as { segments: Segment[], parts: Part[]} _newline_   const { _newline_    currentPartInstance, _newline_    nextPartInstance, _newline_    previousPartInstance _newline_   } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_  _newline_   let currentNextPart: DBPart _newline_   if (nextPartId0) { _newline_    const nextPart = cache.Parts.findOne(nextPartId0) _newline_    if (!nextPart) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part "${nextPartId0}" not found!`) _newline_    currentNextPart = nextPart _newline_   } else { _newline_    const nextPartInstanceTmp = nextPartInstance || currentPartInstance _newline_    if (!nextPartInstanceTmp) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" has no next and no current part!`) _newline_    currentNextPart = nextPartInstanceTmp.part _newline_   } _newline_  _newline_   const currentNextSegment = segments.find(s => s._id === currentNextPart.segmentId) as Segment _newline_   if (!currentNextSegment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${currentNextPart.segmentId}" not found!`) _newline_  _newline_   const partsInSegments: {[segmentId: string]: Part[]} = {} _newline_   _.each(segments, segment => { _newline_    let partsInSegment = _.filter(parts, p => p.segmentId === segment._id) _newline_    if (partsInSegment.length) { _newline_     partsInSegments[unprotectString(segment._id)] = partsInSegment _newline_     parts.push(...partsInSegment) _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  _newline_   let partIndex: number = -1 _newline_   _.find(parts, (part, i) => { _newline_    if (part._id === currentNextPart._id) { _newline_     partIndex = i _newline_     return true _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_   let segmentIndex: number = -1 _newline_   _.find(segments, (s, i) => { _newline_    if (s._id === currentNextSegment._id) { _newline_     segmentIndex = i _newline_     return true _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_   if (partIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part not found in list of parts!`) _newline_   if (segmentIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${currentNextSegment._id}" not found in segmentsWithParts!`) _newline_   if (verticalDelta !== 0) { _newline_    segmentIndex += verticalDelta _newline_  _newline_    const segment = segments[segmentIndex] _newline_    if (!segment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `No Segment found!`) _newline_  _newline_    const part = _.first(partsInSegments[unprotectString(segment._id)]) _newline_    if (!part) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `No Parts in segment "${segment._id}"!`) _newline_  _newline_    partIndex = -1 _newline_    _.find(parts, (p, i) => { _newline_     if (p._id === part._id) { _newline_      partIndex = i _newline_      return true _newline_     } _newline_    }) _newline_    if (partIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part (from segment) not found in list of parts!`) _newline_   } _newline_   partIndex += horizontalDelta _newline_  _newline_   partIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(parts.length - 1, partIndex)) _newline_  _newline_   let part = parts[partIndex] _newline_   if (!part) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `Part index ${partIndex} not found in list of parts!`) _newline_  _newline_   if ((currentPartInstance && part._id === currentPartInstance.part._id && !nextPartId0) || !part.isPlayable()) { _newline_    // Whoops, we're not allowed to next to that. _newline_    // Skip it, then (ie run the whole thing again) _newline_    if (part._id !== nextPartId0) { _newline_     return moveNextPartInner(cache, playlist, horizontalDelta, verticalDelta, setManually, part._id) _newline_    } else { _newline_     // Calling ourselves again at this point would result in an infinite loop _newline_     // There probably isn't any Part available to Next then... _newline_     setNextPartInner(cache, playlist, null, setManually) _newline_     return null _newline_    } _newline_   } else { _newline_    setNextPartInner(cache, playlist, part, setManually) _newline_    return part._id _newline_   } _newline_  } _newline_  export function setNextSegment ( _newline_ (context: MethodContext, rundownPlaylistId: RundownPlaylistId, undo?: boolean) { _newline_   check(rundownPlaylistId, String) _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(rundownPlaylistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_    const dbPlaylist = checkAccessAndGetPlaylist(context, rundownPlaylistId) _newline_  _newline_    if (!dbPlaylist.currentPartInstanceId) throw new Meteor.Error(401, `No current part!`) _newline_  _newline_    const cache = waitForPromise(initCacheForRundownPlaylist(dbPlaylist)) _newline_  _newline_    const playlist = cache.RundownPlaylists.findOne(dbPlaylist._id) as RundownPlaylist _newline_    if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_    const { currentPartInstance, nextPartInstance } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_    if (!currentPartInstance) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `PartInstance "${playlist.currentPartInstanceId}" not found!`) _newline_  _newline_    const rundown = Rundowns.findOne(currentPartInstance.rundownId) _newline_    if (!rundown) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown "${currentPartInstance.rundownId}" not found!`) _newline_    const showStyleBase = rundown.getShowStyleBase() _newline_  _newline_    // @ts-ignore stringify _newline_    // logger.info(o) _newline_    // logger.info(JSON.stringify(o, '', 2)) _newline_  _newline_    const allowedSourceLayers = normalizeArray(showStyleBase.sourceLayers, '_id') _newline_  _newline_    // logger.info('nowInPart', nowInPart) _newline_    // logger.info('filteredPieces', filteredPieces) _newline_    let getNextPiece = (partInstance: PartInstance, undo?: boolean) => { _newline_     // Find next piece to disable _newline_  _newline_     let nowInPart = 0 _newline_     if ( _newline_      partInstance.part.startedPlayback && _newline_      partInstance.part.timings && _newline_      partInstance.part.timings.startedPlayback _newline_     ) { _newline_      let lastStartedPlayback = _.last(partInstance.part.timings.startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_      if (lastStartedPlayback) { _newline_       nowInPart = getCurrentTime() - lastStartedPlayback _newline_      } _newline_     } _newline_  _newline_     const pieceInstances = getAllPieceInstancesFromCache(cache, partInstance) _newline_     const orderedPieces: Array<PieceResolved> = orderPieces(pieceInstances.map(p => p.piece), partInstance.part._id, partInstance.part.getLastStartedPlayback()) _newline_  _newline_     let findLast: boolean = !!undo _newline_  _newline_     let filteredPieces = _.sortBy( _newline_      _.filter(orderedPieces, (piece: PieceResolved) => { _newline_       let sourceLayer = allowedSourceLayers[piece.sourceLayerId] _newline_       if (sourceLayer && sourceLayer.allowDisable && !piece.virtual) return true _newline_       return false _newline_      }), _newline_      (piece: PieceResolved) => { _newline_       let sourceLayer = allowedSourceLayers[piece.sourceLayerId] _newline_       return sourceLayer._rank || -9999 _newline_      } _newline_     ) _newline_     if (findLast) filteredPieces.reverse() _newline_  _newline_     let nextPiece: PieceResolved | undefined = _.find(filteredPieces, (piece) => { _newline_      logger.info('piece.resolvedStart', piece.resolvedStart) _newline_      return ( _newline_       piece.resolvedStart >= nowInPart && _newline_       ( _newline_        ( _newline_         !undo && _newline_         !piece.disabled _newline_        ) || ( _newline_         undo && _newline_         piece.disabled _newline_        ) _newline_       ) _newline_      ) _newline_     }) _newline_     return nextPiece ? pieceInstances.find(p => p.piece._id === nextPiece!._id) : undefined _newline_    } _newline_  _newline_    if (nextPartInstance) { _newline_     // pretend that the next part never has played (even if it has) _newline_     nextPartInstance.part.startedPlayback = false _newline_    } _newline_  _newline_    let partInstances = [ _newline_     currentPartInstance, _newline_     nextPartInstance // If not found in currently playing part, let's look in the next one: _newline_    ] _newline_    if (undo) partInstances.reverse() _newline_  _newline_    let nextPieceInstance: PieceInstance | undefined _newline_  _newline_    _.each(partInstances, (partInstance) => { _newline_     if (partInstance && !nextPieceInstance) { _newline_      nextPieceInstance = getNextPiece(partInstance, undo) _newline_     } _newline_    }) _newline_  _newline_    if (nextPieceInstance) { _newline_     logger.info((undo ? 'Disabling' : 'Enabling') + ' next PieceInstance ' + nextPieceInstance._id) _newline_     cache.PieceInstances.update(nextPieceInstance._id, {$set: { _newline_      'piece.disabled': !undo _newline_     }}) _newline_     // TODO-PartInstance - pending new data flow _newline_     cache.Pieces.update(nextPieceInstance.piece._id, {$set: { _newline_      disabled: !undo _newline_     }}) _newline_  _newline_     updateTimeline(cache, playlist.studioId) _newline_  _newline_     waitForPromise(cache.saveAllToDatabase()) _newline_    } else { _newline_     throw new Meteor.Error(500, 'Found no future pieces') _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_  /** _newline_   * Triggered from Playout-gateway when a Piece has started playing _newline_   */ _newline_  export function _newline_   check(pieceInstanceId, String) _newline_   check(startedPlayback, Number) _newline_  _newline_   triggerWriteAccessBecauseNoCheckNecessary() // tmp _newline_  _newline_   const playlistId _newline_    // This method is called when an auto-next event occurs _newline_  _newline_    const pieceInstance = PieceInstances.findOne({ _newline_     _id: pieceInstanceId, _newline_     rundownId: rundownId _newline_    }) _newline_    if (dynamicallyInserted && !pieceInstance) return// if it was dynamically inserted, it's okay if we can't find it _newline_    if (!pieceInstance) throw new Playout-gateway when a Piece has stopped playing _newline_   */ _newline_  export function onPiecePlaybackStopped (_context: MethodContext, rundownId: RundownId, pieceInstanceId: PieceInstanceId, */ _newline_  export function onPartPlaybackStarted (_context: MethodContext, rundownId: RundownId, partInstanceId: PartInstanceId, startedPlayback: Time) { _newline_   check(rundownId, String) _newline_   check(partInstanceId, String) _newline_   check(startedPlayback, Number) _newline_  _newline_   triggerWriteAccessBecauseNoCheckNecessary() // tmp _newline_  _newline_   const playlistId = getRundown(rundownId).playlistId _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(playlistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_    // This method is called when a part starts playing (like when an auto-next event occurs, or a manual next) _newline_  _newline_    const playingPartInstance = PartInstances.findOne({ _newline_     _id: partInstanceId, _newline_     rundownId: rundownId _newline_    }) _newline_  _newline_    if (playingPartInstance) { _newline_     // make sure we don't run multiple times, even if TSR calls us multiple times _newline_  _newline_   logger.info(`Playout reports PartInstance "${partInstanceId}" has started playback on timestamp ${(new Date(startedPlayback)).toISOString()}`) _newline_  _newline_      const rundown = Rundowns.findOne(rundownId) _newline_      if (!rundown) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown "${rundownId}" not found!`) _newline_      let playlist = RundownPlaylists.findOne(rundown.playlistId) _newline_      if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist "${rundown.playlistId}" not found!`) _newline_      if (!playlist.active) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `Rundown "${rundownId}" is not active!`) _newline_  _newline_      const cache = waitForPromise(initCacheForRundownPlaylist(playlist)) _newline_  _newline_      playlist = cache.RundownPlaylists.findOne(playlist._id) _newline_      if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_      const { currentPartInstance, previousPartInstance } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_  _newline_      if (playlist.currentPartInstanceId === partInstanceId) { _newline_       // this is the current part, it has just started playback _newline_       if (playlist.previousPartInstanceId) { _newline_        if (!previousPartInstance) { _newline_         // We couldn't find the previous part: this is not a critical issue, but is clearly is a symptom of a larger issue _newline_         logger.error(`Previous PartInstance "${playlist.previousPartInstanceId}" on RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" could not be found.`) _newline_        } else if (!previousPartInstance.part.duration) { _newline_         onPartHasStoppedPlaying(cache, previousPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_        } _newline_       } _newline_  _newline_       setRundownStartedPlayback(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_  _newline_       reportPartHasStarted(cache, playingPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_      } else if (playlist.nextPartInstanceId === partInstanceId) { _newline_       // this is the next part, clearly an autoNext has taken place _newline_       if (playlist.currentPartInstanceId) { _newline_        if (!currentPartInstance) { _newline_         // We couldn't find the previous part: this is not a critical issue, but is clearly is a symptom of a larger issue _newline_         logger.error(`Previous PartInstance "${playlist.currentPartInstanceId}" on RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" could not be found.`) _newline_        } else if (!currentPartInstance.part.duration) { _newline_         onPartHasStoppedPlaying(cache, currentPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_        } _newline_       } _newline_  _newline_       setRundownStartedPlayback(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_  _newline_       cache.RundownPlaylists.update(playlist._id, { $set: { _newline_        previousPartInstanceId: playlist.currentPartInstanceId, _newline_        currentPartInstanceId: playingPartInstance._id, _newline_        holdState: RundownHoldState.NONE, _newline_       }}) _newline_  _newline_       reportPartHasStarted(cache, playingPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_       const nextPart = selectNextPart(playlist, playingPartInstance, getAllOrderedPartsFromCache(cache, playlist)) _newline_       libsetNextPart(cache, playlist, nextPart ? nextPart.part : null) _newline_      } else { _newline_       // a part is being played that has not been selected for playback by Core _newline_       // show must go on, so find next part and update the Rundown, but log an error _newline_       const previousReported = playlist.lastIncorrectPartPlaybackReported _newline_  _newline_       if (previousReported && Date.now() - previousReported > INCORRECT_PLAYING_PART_DEBOUNCE) { _newline_        // first time this has happened for a while, let's try to progress the show: _newline_  _newline_        setRundownStartedPlayback(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_  _newline_ _newline_  _newline_ function beforeTake ( _newline_  cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_  partsInOrder: Part[], _newline_  currentPartInstance: PartInstance | null, _newline_  nextPartInstance: PartInstance _newline_ ) { _newline_  // TODO-PartInstance - is this going to work? It needs some work to handle part data changes _newline_  if (currentPartInstance) { _newline_  _newline_  _newline_   const adjacentPart = _.find(partsInOrder, (part) => { _newline_    return ( _newline_     part.segmentId === currentPartInstance.segmentId && _newline_     part._rank > currentPartInstance.part._rank _newline_    ) _newline_   }) _newline_   if (!adjacentPart || adjacentPart._id !== nextPartInstance.part._id) { _newline_    // adjacent Part isn't the next part, do not overflow _newline_    return _newline_   } _newline_   const currentPieces = cache.PieceInstances.findFetch({ partInstanceId: currentPartInstance._id }) _newline_   currentPieces.forEach((instance) => { _newline_    if (instance.piece.overflows && typeof instance.piece.enable.duration === 'number' && instance.piece.enable.duration > 0 && instance.piece.playoutDuration === undefined && instance.piece.userDuration === undefined) { _newline_     // currentPartInstance.part.getLastStartedPlayback() || getCurrentTime()) - getCurrentTime())) _newline_  _newline_     if (remainingDuration > 0) { _newline_      // Clone an overflowing piece _newline_      let overflowedItem = literal<PieceInstance>({ _newline_       _id: getRandomId(), _newline_       rundownId: instance.rundownId, _newline_       partInstanceId: nextPartInstance._id, _newline_       piece: { _newline_        ..._.omit(instance.piece, 'startedPlayback', 'duration', 'overflows'), _newline_        _id: getRandomId(), _newline_        partId: nextPartInstance.part._id, _newline_        enable: { _newline_         start: 0, _newline_         duration: remainingDuration, _newline_        }, _newline_        dynamicallyInserted: true, _newline_        continuesRefId: instance.piece._id, _newline_       } _newline_      }) _newline_  _newline_      cache.PieceInstances.insert(overflowedItem) _newline_  _newline_      // TODO-PartInstance - pending new data flow _newline_      cache.Pieces.insert(overflowedItem.piece) _newline_     } _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_ } _newline_  _newline_ function afterTake ( _newline_  cache: nextPartId _newline_    } _newline_    if (!nextPart) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part "${nextPartId}" not found!`) _newline_   } _newline_  _newline_   libsetNextPart(cache, playlist, nextPart, moveNextPart ( _newline_   context: MethodContext, _newline_   rundownPlaylistId: RundownPlaylistId, _newline_   horizontalDelta: number, _newline_   verticalDelta: number, _newline_   setManually: boolean _newline_  ): PartId | null { _newline_   check(rundownPlaylistId, String) _newline_   check(horizontalDelta, Number) _newline_   check(verticalDelta, Number) _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(rundownPlaylistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_    const dbPlaylist = checkAccessAndGetPlaylist(context, rundownPlaylistId) _newline_    if (!horizontalDelta && !verticalDelta) throw new Meteor.Error(402, `rundownMoveNext: invalid delta: (${horizontalDelta}, ${verticalDelta})`) _newline_  _newline_    const cache = waitForPromise(initCacheForRundownPlaylist(dbPlaylist)) _newline_    const playlist = cache.RundownPlaylists.findOne(dbPlaylist._id) _newline_    if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_    const res = moveNextPartInner( _newline_     cache, _newline_     playlist, _newline_     horizontalDelta, _newline_     verticalDelta, _newline_     setManually _newline_    ) _newline_    waitForPromise(cache.saveAllToDatabase()) _newline_    return res _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_  function moveNextPartInner ( _newline_   cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_   playlist: RundownPlaylist, _newline_   horizontalDelta: number, _newline_   verticalDelta: number, _newline_   setManually: boolean, _newline_   nextPartId0?: PartId _newline_  ): PartId | null { _newline_  _newline_   if (!playlist.active) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" is not active!`) _newline_  _newline_   if (playlist.holdState && playlist.holdState !== RundownHoldState.COMPLETE) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" cannot change next during hold!`) _newline_  _newline_   const { segments, parts } = getSegmentsAndPartsFromCache(cache, playlist) as { segments: Segment[], parts: Part[]} _newline_   const { _newline_    currentPartInstance, _newline_    nextPartInstance, _newline_    previousPartInstance _newline_   } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_  _newline_   let currentNextPart: DBPart _newline_   if (nextPartId0) { _newline_    const nextPart = cache.Parts.findOne(nextPartId0) _newline_    if (!nextPart) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part "${nextPartId0}" not found!`) _newline_    currentNextPart = nextPart _newline_   } else { _newline_    const nextPartInstanceTmp = nextPartInstance || currentPartInstance _newline_    if (!nextPartInstanceTmp) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" has no next and no current part!`) _newline_    currentNextPart = nextPartInstanceTmp.part _newline_   } _newline_  _newline_   const currentNextSegment = segments.find(s => s._id === currentNextPart.segmentId) as Segment _newline_   if (!currentNextSegment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${currentNextPart.segmentId}" not found!`) _newline_  _newline_   const partsInSegments: {[segmentId: string]: Part[]} = {} _newline_   _.each(segments, segment => { _newline_    let partsInSegment = _.filter(parts, p => p.segmentId === segment._id) _newline_    if (partsInSegment.length) { _newline_     partsInSegments[unprotectString(segment._id)] = partsInSegment _newline_     parts.push(...partsInSegment) _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  _newline_   let partIndex: number = -1 _newline_   _.find(parts, (part, i) => { _newline_    if (part._id === currentNextPart._id) { _newline_     partIndex = i _newline_     return true _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_   let segmentIndex: number = -1 _newline_   _.find(segments, (s, i) => { _newline_    if (s._id === currentNextSegment._id) { _newline_     segmentIndex = i _newline_     return true _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_   if (partIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part not found in list of parts!`) _newline_   if (segmentIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${currentNextSegment._id}" not found in segmentsWithParts!`) _newline_   if (verticalDelta !== 0) { _newline_    segmentIndex += verticalDelta _newline_  _newline_    const segment = segments[segmentIndex] _newline_    if (!segment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `No Segment found!`) _newline_  _newline_    const part = _.first(partsInSegments[unprotectString(segment._id)]) _newline_    if (!part) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `No Parts in segment "${segment._id}"!`) _newline_  _newline_    partIndex = -1 _newline_    _.find(parts, (p, i) => { _newline_     if (p._id === part._id) { _newline_      partIndex = i _newline_      return true _newline_     } _newline_    }) _newline_    if (partIndex === -1) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Part (from segment) not found in list of parts!`) _newline_   } _newline_   partIndex += horizontalDelta _newline_  _newline_   partIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(parts.length - 1, partIndex)) _newline_  _newline_   let part = parts[partIndex] _newline_   if (!part) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `Part index ${partIndex} not found in list of parts!`) _newline_  _newline_   if ((currentPartInstance && part._id === currentPartInstance.part._id && !nextPartId0) || !part.isPlayable()) { _newline_    // Whoops, we're not allowed to next to that. _newline_    // Skip it, then (ie run the whole thing again) _newline_    if (part._id !== nextPartId0) { _newline_     return moveNextPartInner(cache, playlist, horizontalDelta, verticalDelta, setManually, part._id) _newline_    } else { _newline_     // Calling ourselves again at this point would result in an infinite loop _newline_     // There probably isn't any Part available to Next then... _newline_     setNextPartInner(cache, playlist, null, setManually) _newline_     return null _newline_    } _newline_   } else { _newline_    setNextPartInner(cache, playlist, part, setManually) _newline_    return part._id _newline_   } _newline_  } _newline_  export function setNextSegment ( _newline_   context: MethodContext, _newline_   rundownPlaylistId: RundownPlaylistId, _newline_   nextSegmentId: SegmentId | null _newline_  ): ClientAPI.ClientResponse<void> { _newline_   check(rundownPlaylistId, String) _newline_   if (nextSegmentId) check(nextSegmentId, String) _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(rundownPlaylistId, `Rundown Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" is not active!`) _newline_  _newline_    const cache = waitForPromise(initCacheForRundownPlaylist(dbPlaylist)) _newline_  _newline_    const playlist = cache.RundownPlaylists.findOne(dbPlaylist._id) _newline_    if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_    let nextSegment: Segment | null = null _newline_    if (nextSegmentId) { _newline_     nextSegment = cache.Segments.findOne(nextSegmentId) || null _newline_  _newline_     if (!nextSegment) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Segment "${nextSegmentId}" not found!`) _newline_     const acceptableRundownIds = getRundownIDsFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_     if _newline_     } _newline_    } _newline_  _newline_    libSetNextSegment(cache, playlist, nextSegment) _newline_  _newline_    waitForPromise(cache.saveAllToDatabase()) _newline_  _newline_    return ClientAPI.responseSuccess(undefined) _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_  export function activateHold (context: MethodContext, rundownPlaylistId: RundownPlaylistId) { _newline_   check(rundownPlaylistId, String) _newline_   logger.debug('rundownActivateHold') _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(rundownPlaylistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT,    if (!dbPlaylist.nextPartInstanceId) throw new Meteor.Error(400, `Rundown Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" no next part!`) _newline_  _newline_    const cache = (context: MethodContext, rundownPlaylistId: RundownPlaylistId, undo?: boolean) { _newline_   check(rundownPlaylistId, String) _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(rundownPlaylistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_    const dbPlaylist = checkAccessAndGetPlaylist(context, rundownPlaylistId) _newline_  _newline_    if (!dbPlaylist.currentPartInstanceId) throw new Meteor.Error(401, `No current part!`) _newline_  _newline_    const cache = waitForPromise(initCacheForRundownPlaylist(dbPlaylist)) _newline_  _newline_    const playlist = cache.RundownPlaylists.findOne(dbPlaylist._id) as RundownPlaylist _newline_    if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist "${rundownPlaylistId}" not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_    const { currentPartInstance, nextPartInstance } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_    if (!currentPartInstance) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `PartInstance "${playlist.currentPartInstanceId}" not found!`) _newline_  _newline_    const rundown = Rundowns.findOne(currentPartInstance.rundownId) _newline_    if (!rundown) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown "${currentPartInstance.rundownId}" not found!`) _newline_    const showStyleBase = rundown.getShowStyleBase() _newline_  _newline_    // @ts-ignore stringify _newline_    // logger.info(o) _newline_    // logger.info(JSON.stringify(o, '', 2)) _newline_  _newline_    const allowedSourceLayers = normalizeArray(showStyleBase.sourceLayers, '_id') _newline_  _newline_    // logger.info('nowInPart', nowInPart) _newline_    // logger.info('filteredPieces', filteredPieces) _newline_    let getNextPiece = (partInstance: PartInstance, undo?: boolean) => { _newline_     // Find next piece to disable _newline_  _newline_     let nowInPart = 0 _newline_     if ( _newline_      partInstance.part.startedPlayback && _newline_      partInstance.part.timings && _newline_      partInstance.part.timings.startedPlayback _newline_     ) { _newline_      let lastStartedPlayback = _.last(partInstance.part.timings.startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_      if (lastStartedPlayback) { _newline_       nowInPart = getCurrentTime() - lastStartedPlayback _newline_      } _newline_     } _newline_  _newline_     const pieceInstances = getAllPieceInstancesFromCache(cache, partInstance) _newline_     const orderedPieces: Array<PieceResolved> = orderPieces(pieceInstances.map(p => p.piece), partInstance.part._id, partInstance.part.getLastStartedPlayback()) _newline_  _newline_     let findLast: boolean = !!undo _newline_  _newline_     let filteredPieces = _.sortBy( _newline_      _.filter(orderedPieces, (piece: PieceResolved) => { _newline_       let sourceLayer = allowedSourceLayers[piece.sourceLayerId] _newline_       if (sourceLayer && sourceLayer.allowDisable && !piece.virtual) return true _newline_       return false _newline_      }), _newline_      (piece: PieceResolved) => { _newline_       let sourceLayer = allowedSourceLayers[piece.sourceLayerId] _newline_       return sourceLayer._rank || -9999 _newline_      } _newline_     ) _newline_     if (findLast) filteredPieces.reverse() _newline_  _newline_     let nextPiece: PieceResolved | undefined = _.find(filteredPieces, (piece) => { _newline_      logger.info('piece.resolvedStart', piece.resolvedStart) _newline_      return ( _newline_       piece.resolvedStart >= nowInPart && _newline_       ( _newline_        ( _newline_         !undo && _newline_         !piece.disabled _newline_        ) || ( _newline_         undo && _newline_         piece.disabled _newline_        ) _newline_       ) _newline_      ) _newline_     }) _newline_     return nextPiece ? pieceInstances.find(p => p.piece._id === nextPiece!._id) : undefined _newline_    } _newline_  _newline_    if (nextPartInstance) { _newline_     // pretend that the next part never has played (even if it has) _newline_     nextPartInstance.part.startedPlayback = false _newline_    } _newline_  _newline_    let partInstances = [ _newline_     currentPartInstance, _newline_     nextPartInstance // If not found in currently playing part, let's look in the next one: _newline_    ] _newline_    if (undo) partInstances.reverse() _newline_  _newline_    let nextPieceInstance: PieceInstance | undefined _newline_  _newline_    _.each(partInstances, (partInstance) => { _newline_     if (partInstance && !nextPieceInstance) { _newline_      nextPieceInstance = getNextPiece(partInstance, undo) _newline_     } _newline_    }) _newline_  _newline_    if (nextPieceInstance) { _newline_     logger.info((undo ? 'Disabling' : 'Enabling') + ' next PieceInstance ' + nextPieceInstance._id) _newline_     cache.PieceInstances.update(nextPieceInstance._id, {$set: { _newline_      'piece.disabled': !undo _newline_     }}) _newline_     // TODO-PartInstance - pending new data flow _newline_     cache.Pieces.update(nextPieceInstance.piece._id, {$set: { _newline_      disabled: !undo _newline_     }}) _newline_  _newline_     updateTimeline(cache, playlist.studioId) _newline_  _newline_     waitForPromise(cache.saveAllToDatabase()) _newline_    } else { _newline_     throw new Meteor.Error(500, 'Found no future pieces') _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_  /** _newline_   * Triggered from Playout-gateway when a Piece has started playing _newline_   */ _newline_  export function _newline_   check(pieceInstanceId, String) _newline_   check(startedPlayback, Number) _newline_  _newline_   triggerWriteAccessBecauseNoCheckNecessary() // tmp _newline_  _newline_   const playlistId = getRundown(rundownId).playlistId _newline_   // TODO - confirm this is correct _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(playlistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_  _newline_    // This method is called when an auto-next event occurs _newline_  _newline_    const pieceInstance = PieceInstances.findOne({ _newline_     _id: pieceInstanceId, _newline_     rundownId: rundownId _newline_    }) _newline_    if (dynamicallyInserted && !pieceInstance) return// if it was dynamically inserted, it's okay if we can't find it _newline_    if (!pieceInstance) throw new Playout-gateway when a Piece has stopped playing _newline_   */ _newline_  export function onPiecePlaybackStopped (_context: MethodContext, rundownId: RundownId, pieceInstanceId: PieceInstanceId, */ _newline_  export function onPartPlaybackStarted (_context: MethodContext, rundownId: RundownId, partInstanceId: PartInstanceId, startedPlayback: Time) { _newline_   check(rundownId, String) _newline_   check(partInstanceId, String) _newline_   check(startedPlayback, Number) _newline_  _newline_   triggerWriteAccessBecauseNoCheckNecessary() // tmp _newline_  _newline_   const playlistId = getRundown(rundownId).playlistId _newline_  _newline_   return rundownPlaylistSyncFunction(playlistId, RundownSyncFunctionPriority.USER_PLAYOUT, () => { _newline_    // This method is called when a part starts playing (like when an auto-next event occurs, or a manual next) _newline_  _newline_    const playingPartInstance = PartInstances.findOne({ _newline_     _id: partInstanceId, _newline_     rundownId: rundownId _newline_    }) _newline_  _newline_    if (playingPartInstance) { _newline_     // make sure we don't run multiple times, even if TSR calls us multiple times _newline_  _newline_   logger.info(`Playout reports PartInstance "${partInstanceId}" has started playback on timestamp ${(new Date(startedPlayback)).toISOString()}`) _newline_  _newline_      const rundown = Rundowns.findOne(rundownId) _newline_      if (!rundown) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown "${rundownId}" not found!`) _newline_      let playlist = RundownPlaylists.findOne(rundown.playlistId) _newline_      if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist "${rundown.playlistId}" not found!`) _newline_      if (!playlist.active) throw new Meteor.Error(501, `Rundown "${rundownId}" is not active!`) _newline_  _newline_      const cache = waitForPromise(initCacheForRundownPlaylist(playlist)) _newline_  _newline_      playlist = cache.RundownPlaylists.findOne(playlist._id) _newline_      if (!playlist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown Playlist not found in cache!`) _newline_  _newline_      const { currentPartInstance, previousPartInstance } = getSelectedPartInstancesFromCache(cache, playlist) _newline_  _newline_      if (playlist.currentPartInstanceId === partInstanceId) { _newline_       // this is the current part, it has just started playback _newline_       if (playlist.previousPartInstanceId) { _newline_        if (!previousPartInstance) { _newline_         // We couldn't find the previous part: this is not a critical issue, but is clearly is a symptom of a larger issue _newline_         logger.error(`Previous PartInstance "${playlist.previousPartInstanceId}" on RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" could not be found.`) _newline_        } else if (!previousPartInstance.part.duration) { _newline_         onPartHasStoppedPlaying(cache, previousPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_        } _newline_       } _newline_  _newline_       setRundownStartedPlayback(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_  _newline_       reportPartHasStarted(cache, playingPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_      } else if (playlist.nextPartInstanceId === partInstanceId) { _newline_       // this is the next part, clearly an autoNext has taken place _newline_       if (playlist.currentPartInstanceId) { _newline_        if (!currentPartInstance) { _newline_         // We couldn't find the previous part: this is not a critical issue, but is clearly is a symptom of a larger issue _newline_         logger.error(`Previous PartInstance "${playlist.currentPartInstanceId}" on RundownPlaylist "${playlist._id}" could not be found.`) _newline_        } else if (!currentPartInstance.part.duration) { _newline_         onPartHasStoppedPlaying(cache, currentPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_        } _newline_       } _newline_  _newline_       setRundownStartedPlayback(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_  _newline_       cache.RundownPlaylists.update(playlist._id, { $set: { _newline_        previousPartInstanceId: playlist.currentPartInstanceId, _newline_        currentPartInstanceId: playingPartInstance._id, _newline_        holdState: RundownHoldState.NONE, _newline_       }}) _newline_  _newline_       reportPartHasStarted(cache, playingPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_       const nextPart = selectNextPart(playlist, playingPartInstance, getAllOrderedPartsFromCache(cache, playlist)) _newline_       libsetNextPart(cache, playlist, nextPart ? nextPart.part : null) _newline_      } else { _newline_       // a part is being played that has not been selected for playback by Core _newline_       // show must go on, so find next part and update the Rundown, but log an error _newline_       const previousReported = playlist.lastIncorrectPartPlaybackReported _newline_  _newline_       if (previousReported && Date.now() - previousReported > INCORRECT_PLAYING_PART_DEBOUNCE) { _newline_        // first time this has happened for a while, let's try to progress the show: _newline_  _newline_        setRundownStartedPlayback(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_  _newline_        cache.RundownPlaylists.update(playlist._id, { $set: { _newline_         previousPartInstanceId: null, _newline_         currentPartInstanceId: playingPartInstance._id, _newline_         lastIncorrectPartPlaybackReported: Date.now() // save the time to prevent the system to go in a loop _newline_        }}) _newline_  _newline_        reportPartHasStarted(cache, playingPartInstance, startedPlayback) _newline_  _newline_        const nextPart = selectNextPart(playlist, playingPartInstance, getAllOrderedPartsFromCache(cache, playlist)) _newline_        libsetNextPart(cache, playlist, nextPart ? nextPart.part : null) _newline_       } _newline_  _newline_       // TODO - should this even change the next? _newline_       logger.error(`PartInstance "${playingPartInstance._id}" has started playback by the playout gateway, but has not been selected for playback!`) _newline_      } _newline_  _newline_      // Load the latest data and complete the take _newline_      const rundownPlaylist = RundownPlaylists.findOne(rundown.playlistId) _newline_      if (!rundownPlaylist) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `RundownPlaylist "${rundown.playlistId}", parent of rundown "${rundown._id}" not found!`) _newline_  _newline_      afterTake(cache, rundownPlaylist, playingPartInstance) _newline_  _newline_      waitForPromise(cache.saveAllToDatabase()) _newline_     } _newline_    } else { _newline_     throw new Meteor.Error(404, `PartInstance "${partInstanceId}" in rundown "${rundownId}" not found!`) _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  } ( _newline_   context: MethodContext, _newline_   rundownPlaylistId: RundownPlaylistId, _newline_   partInstanceId: PartInstanceId, _newline_   property: string, _newline_   value: string _newline_  )     delete rArguments[property] _newline_    } else { _newline_     // set property _newline_     const mSet: any = {} _newline_     const mSet1: any = {} _newline_     mSet['runtimeArguments.' + property] = value _newline_     mSet1['part.runtimeArguments.' + property] = value _newline_     mSet.dirty = true _newline_     cache.Parts.update(partInstance.part._id, { $set: mSet }) _newline_     cache.PartInstances.update(partInstance._id, { $set: mSet1 }) _newline_  _newline_     rArguments[property] = value _newline_    } _newline_  _newline_    waitForPromise(refreshPart(cache, rundown, _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_  /** _newline_   * Called from Playout-gateway when the trigger-time of a timeline object has updated _newline_   * ( typically when using the "now"-feature ) _newline_  _newline_  export function shouldUpdateStudioBaseline (context: MethodContext, studioId: StudioId) { _newline_   StudioContentWriteAccess.baseline(context, studioId) _newline_   let cache: _newline_   check(studioId, String) _newline_  _newline_   const studio = cache.Studios.findOne(studioId) _newline_  _newline_   if (!studio) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Studio "${studioId}" not 'coreVersion' _newline_  _newline_    if (versionsContent.studio !== (studio._rundownVersionHash || 0)) return 'studio' _newline_  _newline_    if (versionsContent.blueprintId !== studio.blueprintId) _newline_ function checkAccessAndGetPlaylist (context: MethodContext, playlistId: RundownPlaylistId): RundownPlaylist { _newline_  const access = RundownPlaylistContentWriteAccess.playout(context, _newline_  _newline_ function beforeTake ( _newline_  cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_  partsInOrder: Part[], _newline_  currentPartInstance: PartInstance | null, _newline_  nextPartInstance: PartInstance _newline_ ) { _newline_  // TODO-PartInstance - is this going to work? It needs some work to handle part data changes _newline_  if (currentPartInstance) { _newline_  _newline_  _newline_   const adjacentPart = _.find(partsInOrder, (part) => { _newline_    return ( _newline_     part.segmentId === currentPartInstance.segmentId && _newline_     part._rank > currentPartInstance.part._rank _newline_    ) _newline_   }) _newline_   if (!adjacentPart || adjacentPart._id !== nextPartInstance.part._id) { _newline_    // adjacent Part isn't the next part, do not overflow _newline_    return _newline_   } _newline_   const currentPieces = cache.PieceInstances.findFetch({ partInstanceId: currentPartInstance._id }) _newline_   currentPieces.forEach((instance) => { _newline_    if (instance.piece.overflows && typeof instance.piece.enable.duration === 'number' && instance.piece.enable.duration > 0 && instance.piece.playoutDuration === undefined && instance.piece.userDuration === undefined) { _newline_     // Subtract the amount played from the duration _newline_     const remainingDuration = Math.max(0, instance.piece.enable.duration - ((instance.piece.startedPlayback || currentPartInstance.part.getLastStartedPlayback() || getCurrentTime()) - getCurrentTime())) _newline_  _newline_     if (remainingDuration > 0) { _newline_      // Clone an overflowing piece _newline_      let overflowedItem = literal<PieceInstance>({ _newline_       _id: getRandomId(), _newline_       rundownId: instance.rundownId, _newline_       partInstanceId: nextPartInstance._id, _newline_       piece: { _newline_        ..._.omit(instance.piece, 'startedPlayback', 'duration', 'overflows'), _newline_        _id: getRandomId(), _newline_        partId: nextPartInstance.part._id, _newline_        enable: { _newline_         start: 0, _newline_         duration: remainingDuration, _newline_        }, _newline_        dynamicallyInserted: true, _newline_        continuesRefId: instance.piece._id, _newline_       } _newline_      }) _newline_  _newline_      cache.PieceInstances.insert(overflowedItem) _newline_  _newline_      // TODO-PartInstance - pending new data flow _newline_      cache.Pieces.insert(overflowedItem.piece) _newline_     } _newline_    } _newline_   }) _newline_  } _newline_ } _newline_  _newline_ function afterTake ( _newline_  cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_  playlist: RundownPlaylist, _newline_  takePartInstance: PartInstance, _newline_  timeOffset: number | null = null _newline_ ) { _newline_  // This function should be called at the end of a "take" event (when the Parts have been updated) _newline_  _newline_  let forceNowTime: number | undefined = undefined _newline_  if (timeOffset) { _newline_   forceNowTime = getCurrentTime() - timeOffset _newline_  } _newline_  // or after a new part has started playing _newline_  updateTimeline(cache, playlist.studioId, forceNowTime) _newline_  _newline_  // defer these so that the playout gateway has the chance to learn about the changes _newline_  Meteor.setTimeout(() => { // todo _newline_   if (takePartInstance.part.shouldNotifyCurrentPlayingPart) { _newline_    const currentRundown = Rundowns.findOne(takePartInstance.rundownId) _newline_    if (!currentRundown) throw new Meteor.Error(404, `Rundown "${takePartInstance.rundownId}" of partInstance "${takePartInstance._id}" not found`) _newline_    IngestActions.notifyCurrentPlayingPart(currentRundown, takePartInstance.part) _newline_   } _newline_  }, 40) _newline_ } _newline_  _newline_ function setRundownStartedPlayback ( _newline_  cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, _newline_  playlist: RundownPlaylist, _newline_  rundown: Rundown, _newline_  startedPlayback: Time _newline_ ) { _newline_  if (!rundown.startedPlayback) { // Set startedPlayback on the rundown if this is the first item to be played _newline_   reportRundownHasStarted(cache, playlist, rundown, startedPlayback) _newline_  } _newline_ } _newline_  _newline_ interface UpdateTimelineFromIngestDataTimeout { _newline_  timeout?: number _newline_  changedSegments: SegmentId[] _newline_ } _newline_ let updateTimelineFromIngestDataTimeouts: { _newline_  [rundownId: string]: UpdateTimelineFromIngestDataTimeout _newline_ } = {} _newline_ export function triggerUpdateTimelineAfterIngestData (cache: CacheForRundownPlaylist, rundownId: RundownId, changedSegmentIds: SegmentId[]) { _newline_  // Lock behind a timeout, so it doesnt get executed loads when importing a rundown or there are large changes _newline_  let data: UpdateTimelineFromIngestDataTimeout = updateTimelineFromIngestDataTimeouts[unprotectString(rundownId)] _newline_  if (data) { _newline_   if (data.timeout) Meteor.clearTimeout(data.timeout) _newline_   data.changedSegments = data.changedSegments.concat(changedSegmentIds) _newline_  } else { _newline_   data = { _newline_    changedSegments: changedSegmentIds _newline_   } _newline_  } _newline_  _newline_  data.timeout = Meteor.setTimeout(() => { _newline_   delete updateTimelineFromIngestDataTimeouts[unprotectString(rundownId)] _newline_  _newline_   // infinite items only need to be recalculated for those after where the edit was made (including the edited line) _newline_   let prevPart: Part | undefined _newline_   if (data.changedSegments) { _newline_    const firstSegment = cache.Segments.findOne({ _newline_     rundownId: rundownId, _newline_     _id: { $in: data.changedSegments } _newline_    }) _newline_    if (firstSegment) { _newline_     prevPart = getPartBeforeSegment(rundownId, firstSegment) _newline_    } _newline_   } _newline_  _newline_   const rundown = cache.Rundowns.findOne(rundownId) _newline_
